与版本差异）。\n参考截图：github-repository-overview.png、github-commit-history.png、github-commit-diff.png。\n视觉复核结论（本 Issue 三图 + 缺口）已登记 `docs/ui-reference-notes.md`；文件浏览无独立视觉参考（截图与 overview 重复），布局按需求语义实现。\n\n## 共享决定（必须遵循）\ndocs/architecture.md §5 内容模型（commit 共享不可变、branch=指针、tree_json 快照、commit_files）、**§7.2 代码浏览/历史/版本比较契约（M4a 登记）**、§9.1/9.2/§9.16（M4a 决定条目，合入时取当时最小空闲号。设计期登记为 §9.14，合入 develop 时按“后合入者让号”由 M4a 内容种子）。\n本模块交付的 diff/树 API 是 M6a 比较页与 Files changed 的直接依赖：API 形状（base/compare → 变更文件列表 + 每文件 additions/deletions + 行级 diff）一经发布在 Issue 评论登记为准。\n\n## 共享契约（M4a 登记，权威入口；#7/#9/#10 消费）\n\n页面路由（在 §7.1 之上扩展，不新建第二套）：\n- `/:owner/:repo`、`/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*`：Code 页/目录页；页面上唯一历史链接 accessible name 恰为 `Commits`（计数为 `aria-hidden` 徽标，可见 “Commits 2”）；另提供最小只读 `Branch` combobox 切换分支（REQ-4-1 THEN2 需要）。\n- `/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*`：文件页；`Commits` 指向该 path 的范围历史；“最近 commit” = 最后触碰该 path 的提交。\n- `/:owner/:repo/commits[/:branch][/*path]`：分支/文件范围历史，`created_at DESC, id DESC`；`/:owner/:repo/commit/:rev[/<path>]`：commit diff 页（base = 第一父）。\n- `/:owner/:repo/compare?base=&compare=`：revision 比较页（全 viewer 可读；combobox `base`/`compare`，选项为 commit 短 hash；按钮 `Compare changes`）。REQ-6-2-2 的 PR 比较流由 M6a 自建页面（Write+、分支选项），复用同一 diff 组件与 `/api/.../compare`。\n\n数据端点（`/api/repos/:owner/:name` 前缀；全部经 `loadRepo` + `requireViewable`；不可见仓库 / 未知 branch、path、rev → `404 not_found`；只读）：\n- `GET /contents?branch=&path=`（§7.1 形状；M4a 更正约定 4/5：`commit` = 最后触碰该 path 的提交；空分支 → 200 空目录）\n- `GET /branches` → `{branches:[{name,isDefault,headCommitId,shortId}]}`\n- `GET /commits?branch=&path=&limit=` → `{branch,path,count,commits:[{id,shortId,message,author,createdAt,parentId,parentShortId,changedFiles:[{path,status,additions,deletions}],url}]}`\n- `GET /commits/:rev` → `{commit:{…}}`\n- `GET /commits/:rev/diff?base=&path=` → diff 载荷\n- `GET /compare?base=&compare=&path=` → diff 载荷：\n  `{base:{rev,id,shortId,label,message,createdAt}|null, compare:{…}, mergeBase:{id,shortId}|null, commits:[{id,shortId,message,author,createdAt,url}], files:[{path,status,additions,deletions,url,lines:[{type,oldNumber,newNumber,text}]}], totals:{files,additions,deletions}, empty}`\n\n约定：\n1. `rev`/`base`/`compare` 接受 7 位 hex 短 hash（`id` 的 16 进制左补零；形如 `/^[0-9a-f]{7}$/i` 优先按 hex 解析）→ 十进制 id → 分支名；解析域 = **本仓库分支可达 commit 集**（fork 直接读共享不可变 commit，§9.1）。短 hash 由 id 派生，不新增 `sha` 列。\n2. `compare` = **merge-base(base,compare) → compare**（三点）；`commits[]` = compare 可达、merge base 不可达（M6a 的 comparable commits / 空差异判定），`lines[].oldNumber/newNumber` 供 M6b 行内评论锚定。\n3. `commit_files` 与 tree diff 必须一致（seed 用同一行 diff 助手计算 + 全量 Vitest 断言）；服务端以 tree diff 为事实来源。\n4. 渲染/文案约束（影响自动验收）：消息/作者/相对时间/文件路径各自独立文本节点；增删数为可见文本；历史页短 hash 为唯一链接、diff 页 base/compare 用文本；diff 行的代码文本、`aria-hidden` 的 `+`/`-`、视觉隐藏的 `Added`/`Deleted`/`Unchanged` 各自成节点。\n5. 跨任务边界（根 Issue #1 复核确认，本 Issue comment #96）：最小只读分支端点 + `Branch` combobox 归 M4a（REQ-4-1 THEN2 的验收场景在 M4a 范围内，不等 #7）；#7 在**唯一** branches 端点上扩展/替换为 REQ-4-3-1 的 `Branch <name>` + `Find branch`，不得另起第二套端点；替换控件须保持 THEN2 可观察，断言形态变化按契约变更纪律在 #7 PR 内提出并同步 acceptance-plan。`main-only.md` 是 REQ-4-1 THEN2 的唯一种子证人，#9 的 PR 塑形不得移除它。\n\n## 交付\n1. 文件浏览：Code 页目录列表（目录/文件条目为链接，accessible name = 各自名称）、路径面包屑（可返回父目录）、当前分支与路径；文件页显示完整存储内容（完整文本值可见）、最近 commit、文件名；刷新保持分支/路径/内容。\n2. 提交历史：仓库与文件页各一个历史链接 \"Commits\"（可带计数）；历史页按时间倒序，含 short hash、消息、作者、相对时间（含 \"ago\"）；文件范围历史只显示改动该文件的 commit；点击 commit 进详情（parent、changed files 保持可见）。\n3. commit diff：diff 页显示 base/compare 标识、变更文件列表、每文件增删行；新增/删除行可被读屏区分；\"Changed files\" 汇总与数值统计；未变更文件不出现；只读不产生写操作。\n4. 后端：树/commit/diff/compare/branches API + Vitest（快照一致性、diff 计算、权限：无 view 权限不返回内容）。\n5. seed：acme-docs 的 README.md/src/search.ts/docs/、main+feature-search 分支、\"Document search flow\" commit（幂等）；commit 显式递进 `created_at`；`commit_files` 增删数由行 diff 计算。\n6. 最小只读分支切换（`GET /branches` + `Branch` combobox）：满足 REQ-4-1 THEN2 的验收必需项，完整分支能力归 #7。\n\n## 前置\nM3（仓库概览/创建）已合入 develop（仓库对象与权限判定可用）。\n\n## 验收\nPlaywright 覆盖 3 条需求场景；种子内容断言（完整文本值、\"ago\" 时间、面包屑返回）；刷新持久化；\n必测反例：`docs/guide.md` 文件范围历史不含 `Document search flow`；`feature-search` 历史含 `Add main-only notes`（changed files = added+modified）；私有仓库短 hash 直开 diff 无内容泄露；compare 同 revision → 与版本差异）。\n参考截图：github-repository-overview.png、github-commit-history.png、github-commit-diff.png。\n视觉复核结论（本 Issue 三图 + 缺口）已登记 `docs/ui-reference-notes.md`；文件浏览无独立视觉参考（截图与 overview 重复），布局按需求语义实现。\n\n## 内容种子）。\n本模块交付的 diff/树 API 是 M6a 比较页与 Files changed 的直接依赖：API 形状（base/compare → 变更文件列表 + 每文件 additions/deletions + 行级 diff）一经发布在 Issue 评论登记为准。\n\n## 共享契约（M4a 登记，权威入口；#7/#9/#10 消费）\n\n页面路由（在 §7.1 之上扩展，不新建第二套）：\n- `/:owner/:repo`、`/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*`：Code 页/目录页；页面上唯一历史链接 accessible name 恰为 `Commits`（计数为 `aria-hidden` 徽标，可见 “Commits 2”）；另提供最小只读 `Branch` combobox 切换分支（REQ-4-1 THEN2 需要）。\n- `/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*`：文件页；`Commits` 指向该 path 的范围历史；“最近 commit” = 最后触碰该 path 的提交。\n- `/:owner/:repo/commits[/:branch][/*path]`：分支/文件范围历史，`created_at DESC, id DESC`；`/:owner/:repo/commit/:rev[/<path>]`：commit diff 页（base = 第一父）。\n- `/:owner/:repo/compare?base=&compare=`：revision 比较页（全 viewer 可读；combobox `base`/`compare`，选项为 commit 短 hash；按钮 `Compare changes`）。REQ-6-2-2 的 PR 比较流由 M6a 自建页面（Write+、分支选项），复用同一 diff 组件与 `/api/.../compare`。\n\n数据端点（`/api/repos/:owner/:name` 前缀；全部经 `loadRepo` + `requireViewable`；不可见仓库 / 未知 branch、path、rev → `404 not_found`；只读）：\n- `GET /contents?branch=&path=`（§7.1 形状；M4a 更正约定 4/5：`commit` = 最后触碰该 path 的提交；空分支 → 200 空目录）\n- `GET /branches` → `{branches:[{name,isDefault,headCommitId,shortId}]}`\n- `GET /commits?branch=&path=&limit=` → `{branch,path,count,commits:[{id,shortId,message,author,createdAt,parentId,parentShortId,changedFiles:[{path,status,additions,deletions}],url}]}`\n- `GET /commits/:rev` → `{commit:{…}}`\n- `GET /commits/:rev/diff?base=&path=` → diff 载荷\n- `GET /compare?base=&compare=&path=` → diff 载荷：\n  `{base:{rev,id,shortId,label,message,createdAt}|null, compare:{…}, mergeBase:{id,shortId}|null, commits:[{id,shortId,message,author,createdAt,url}], files:[{path,status,additions,deletions,url,lines:[{type,oldNumber,newNumber,text}]}], totals:{files,additions,deletions}, empty}`\n\n约定：\n1. `rev`/`base`/`compare` 接受 7 位 hex 短 hash（`id` 的 16 进制左补零；形如 `/^[0-9a-f]{7}$/i` 优先按 hex 解析）→ 十进制 id → 分支名；解析域 = **本仓库分支可达 commit 集**（fork 直接读共享不可变 commit，§9.1）。短 hash 由 id 派生，不新增 `sha` 列。\n2. `compare` = **merge-base(base,compare) → compare**（三点）；`commits[]` = compare 可达、merge base 不可达（M6a 的 comparable commits / 空差异判定），`lines[].oldNumber/newNumber` 供 M6b 行内评论锚定。\n3. `commit_files` 与 tree diff 必须一致（seed 用同一行 diff 助手计算 + 全量 Vitest 断言）；服务端以 tree diff 为事实来源。\n4. 渲染/文案约束（影响自动验收）：消息/作者/相对时间/文件路径各自独立文本节点；增删数为可见文本；历史页短 hash 为唯一链接、diff 页 base/compare 用文本；diff 行的代码文本、`aria-hidden` 的 `+`/`-`、视觉隐藏的 `Added`/`Deleted`/`Unchanged` 各自成节点。\n5. 跨任务边界（根 Issue #1 复核确认，本 Issue comment #96）：最小只读分支端点 + `Branch` combobox 归 M4a（REQ-4-1 THEN2 的验收场景在 M4a 范围内，不等 #7）；#7 在**唯一** branches 端点上扩展/替换为 REQ-4-3-1 的 `Branch <name>` + `Find branch`，不得另起第二套端点；替换控件须保持 THEN2 可观察，断言形态变化按契约变更纪律在 #7 PR 内提出并同步 acceptance-plan。`main-only.md` 是 REQ-4-1 THEN2 的唯一种子证人，#9 的 PR 塑形不得移除它。\n\n## 交付\n1. 文件浏览：Code 页目录列表（目录/文件条目为链接，accessible name = 各自名称）、路径面包屑（可返回父目录）、当前分支与路径；文件页显示完整存储内容（完整文本值可见）、最近 commit、文件名；刷新保持分支/路径/内容。\n2. 提交历史：仓库与文件页各一个历史链接 \"Commits\"（可带计数）；历史页按时间倒序，含 short hash、消息、作者、相对时间（含 \"ago\"）；文件范围历史只显示改动该文件的 commit；点击 commit 进详情（parent、changed files 保持可见）。\n3. commit diff：diff 页显示 base/compare 标识、变更文件列表、每文件增删行；新增/删除行可被读屏区分；\"Changed files\" 汇总与数值统计；未变更文件不出现；只读不产生写操作。\n4. 后端：树/commit/diff/compare/branches API + Vitest（快照一致性、diff 计算、权限：无 view 权限不返回内容）。\n5. seed：acme-docs 的 README.md/src/search.ts/docs/、main+feature-search 分支、\"Document search flow\" commit（幂等）；commit 显式递进 `created_at`；`commit_files` 增删数由行 共享基线（裁决全文：issue #5 评论 #42，经 requirements 原文核实 + advisor 独立审查）。登记按下列修订版生效，**docs 契约文本由 PR #11 写入 architecture.md 后随合入发布**：\n\n1. 页面路由：`/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*path`（文件）、`/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*path`（目录）。\n2. 数据 API：`GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/contents?branch=&path=` → `{contents:{type,branch,path,name?,content?,commit?,entries:[{name,type,path,url}]}}`；不可见仓库与未知 branch/path → 404。\n3. **补充约定（裁决新增，M4a 实施时必须遵守）**：\n   - 编码规则：`:branch` 恒为单 URL 段（`encodeURIComponent(branch)`，分支名可含 `/` 如 `feature/api-v2`）；`*path` 按段编码后以 `/` 连接，禁止原始拼接；`entries[].url` 已完整编码，消费时不得二次编码。\n   - 裸路由语义：`/:owner/:repo` 恒为默认分支根列表；分支切换 = 导航到 `/tree/<branch>[/<path>]`（分支必须在 URL 中，REQ-4-3-1/4-3-2 刷新持久化判据强制）。\n   - **约定 4（归 M4a 实现）**：contents 的 `commit` 字段目标语义为\"最后触碰该 path 的提交\"（REQ-4-1 原文）；M3 现状返回分支头 commit，是已登记近似，M4a 负责更正。\n   - **约定 5（归 M4a 实现）**：存在但无提交的分支返回 `{type:'directory', entries:[]}`（200）；仅未知 branch/path → 404。M3 现状为空分支 404，M4a 负责更正。\n4. diff API 形状由 M4a 登记定义，但**必须以 commitId 为键**（§9.1，M6a/#9 硬依赖）。\n\n指派后请负责人在实施计划中确认消费本契约，并把约定 4/5 的更正纳入范围。\n\n\ncomment #84 [visible]\nthread: 84 [open]\n## 指派交接（@deepseek-9，根负责人 @glm-1）\n\nM4a 依赖已就绪：#5（M3/PR #11）已合入 develop，工作基线 **origin/develop @ `53532a0`**（含 M1/PR #12 + M3 全部实现与 docs）。请先         "text": "/**\n * Commit history and revision algebra over the content model\n * (docs/architecture.md §5 content model, §7.2 conventions 1/2/6).\n *\n * This module is deliberately free of repository-module imports so the seed and\n * `repos/service.js` (convention 4 — \"commit that last touched a path\") can reuse\n * it without cycles. All queries are read-only.\n */\nimport { diffTrees, treeChangeSet, changeSetTouchesPath } from './diff.js';\n\n/** 7-character hex short hash derived from the numeric commit id (stable forever). */\nexport function shortId(commitId) {\n  return Number(commitId).toString(16).padStart(7, '0');\n}\n\nexport function readTree(treeJson) {\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(treeJson || '{}');\n  } catch {\n    return {};\n  }\n}\n\nfunction treeOf(db, commitId) {\n  if (commitId == null) return {};\n  const row = db.prepare('SELECT tree_json FROM commits WHERE id = ?').get(commitId);\n  return readTree(row?.tree_json);\n}\n\n/** All parent ids of a commit (first parent from `commits.parent_id`, merges from `commit_parents`). */\nexport function parentIds(db, commitId) {\n  const rows = db\n    .prepare('SELECT parent_commit_id AS id FROM commit_parents WHERE commit_id = ? ORDER BY position')\n    .all(commitId);\n  if (rows.length) return rows.map((row) => row.id);\n  const commit = db.prepare('SELECT parent_id FROM commits WHERE id = ?').get(commitId);\n  return commit?.parent_id != null ? [commit.parent_id] : [];\n}\n\n/**\n * Ancestors of a commit, itself included (`{id: commitRow}`). Follows every\n * parent (§9.2 second parents participate in reachability).\n */\nexport function ancestorsOrSelf(db, commitId) {\n  const visited = new Map();\n  const queue = commitId != null ? [commitId] : [];\n  while (queue.length) {\n    const id = queue.pop();\n    if (visited.has(id)) continue;\n    const commit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM commits WHERE id = ?').get(id);\n    if (!commit) continue;\n    visited.set(id, commit);\n    queue.push(...parentIds(db, id));\n  }\n  return visited;\n}\n\n/**\n * Commits reachable from any branch of this repository (docs/architecture.md\n * §7.2 convention 1). Forks share the immutable commit rows of their source, so\n * resolving against branch reachability — not `commits.repo_id` — is what makes\n * history/diff work on forks (§9.1).\n * @returns {Map<number, object>} commit id -> commit row\n */\nexport function reachableCommits(db, repo) {\n  const branches = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM branches WHERE repo_id = ?').all(repo.id);\n  const reachable = new Map();\n  for (const branch of branches) {\n    if (branch.head_commit_id == null) continue;\n    for (const [id, commit] of ancestorsOrSelf(db, branch.head_commit_id)) reachable.set(id, commit);\n  }\n  return reachable;\n}\n\n/**\n * Merge base of two commits (§9.2): the common ancestor that is farthest from\n * the compare side (first hit in a breadth-first walk from `compareId`).\n */\nexport function mergeBase(db, baseId, compareId) {\n  if (baseId == null || compareId == null) return null;\n  const baseAncestors = ancestorsOrSelf(db, baseId);\n  const visited = new Set();\n  let queue = [compareId];\n  while (queue.length) {\n    const next = [];\n    for (const id of queue) {\n      if (visited.has(id)) continue;\n      visited.add(id);\n      if (baseAncestors.has(id)) return id;\n      next.push(...parentIds(db, id));\n    }\n    queue = next;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Revision resolution (docs/architecture.md §7.2 convention 1), in order:\n * 7-char hex short hash → decimal commit id → exact branch name; a miss falls\n * through to the next rule. The resolution domain is the commit set reachable\n * from this repository's branches.\n * @returns {{commit: object, label: string}|null}\n */\nexport function resolveRev(db, repo, rawRev) {\n  const rev = String(rawRev ?? '').trim();\n  if (!rev) return null;\n  const reachable = reachableCommits(db, repo);\n  const branchByHead = new Map();\n  for (const branch of db.prepare('SELECT * FROM branches WHERE repo_id = ?').all(repo.id)) {\n    if (branch.head_commit_id != null && !branchByHead.has(branch.head_commit_id)) {\n      branchByHead.set(branch.head_commit_id, branch.name);\n    }\n  }\n  const labelFor = (commit) => branchByHead.get(commit.id) ?? shortId(commit.id);\n\n  if (/^[0-9a-f]{7}$/i.test(rev)) {\n    for (const commit of reachable.values()) {\n      if (shortId(commit.id) === rev.toLowerCase()) return { commit, label: labelFor(commit) };\n    }\n  }\n  if (/^\\d+$/.test(rev)) {\n    const commit = reachable.get(Number(rev));\n    if (commit) return { commit, label: labelFor(commit) };\n  }\n  const branch = db\n    .prepare('SELECT * FROM branches WHERE repo_id = ? AND name = ?')\n    .get(repo.id, rev);\n  if (branch?.head_commit_id != null) {\n    const commit = reachable.get(branch.head_commit_id);\n    if (commit) return { commit, label: branch.name };\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** First-parent change set of a commit (what the commit itself changed, §7.2 convention 6). */\nexport function changeSetOf(db, commit) {\n  const parents = parentIds(db, commit.id);\n  const firstParent = parents.length ? parents[0] : null;\n  return treeChangeSet(treeOf(db, firstParent), readTree(commit.tree_json));\n}\n\n/**\n * Commits of one branch, newest first (`created_at DESC, id DESC`), optionally\n * restricted to the commits whose first-parent change set touches `path`\n * (file/directory scope, §7.2 convention 6).\n */\nexport function branchHistory(db, repo, branchName, { path = '', limit = 100 } = {}) {\n  const branch = db\n    .prepare('SELECT * FROM branches WHERE repo_id = ? AND name = ?')\n    .get(repo.id, branchName);\n  if (!branch) return null;\n  const commits = ancestorsOrSelf(db, branch.head_commit_id);\n  const ordered = [...commits.values()].sort((a, b) => {\n    if (a.created_at !== b.created_at) return a.created_at < b.created_at ? 1 : -1;\n    return b.id - a.id;\n  });\n  const trimmed = path\n    ? ordered.filter((commit) => changeSetTouchesPath(changeSetOf(db, commit), path))\n    : ordered;\n  return { branch, commits: trimmed.slice(0, limit), total: trimmed.length };\n}\n\n/**\n * The last commit whose first-parent change set touches `path` on `branchName`\n * (docs/architecture.md §7.1 convention 4). Returns `null` when the branch is\n * unknown or no commit in its history touches the path.\n */\nexport function lastCommitTouchingPath(db, repo, branchName, path) {\n  const history = branchHistory(db, repo, branchName, { path, limit: Infinity });\n  if (!history) return null;\n  return history.commits[0] ?? null;\n}\n\n/** Line-level diff between two commits (or between `baseId` trees directly). */\nexport function diffCommits(db, baseId, compareCommit, path = '') {\n  const baseTree = treeOf(db, baseId);\n  const compareTree = readTree(compareCommit.tree_json);\n  const files = diffTrees(baseTree, compareTree);\n  const scoped = path\n    ? files.filter((file) => file.path === path || file.path.startsWith(`${path}/`))\n    : files;\n  const totals = scoped.reduce(\n    (accumulator, file) => ({\n      additions: accumulator.additions + file.additions,\n      deletions: accumulator.deletions + file.deletions,\n    }),\n    { additions: 0, deletions: 0 },\n  );\n  return {\n    files: scoped.map(({ path: filePath, status, additions, deletions, lines }) => ({\n      path: filePath,\n      status,\n      additions,\n      deletions,\n      lines,\n    })),\n    totals: { files: scoped.length, ...totals },\n    empty: scoped.length === 0,\n  };\n}"

        "text": "/**\n * Line-level diff over `tree_json` snapshots (docs/architecture.md §5, §7.2).\n *\n * A tree is a full `{path: content}` snapshot, so the difference between two\n * revisions is a pure function of the two trees. The same helper is used by the\n * runtime (history/diff/compare endpoints) and by the seed (commit_files\n * additions/deletions), which keeps `commit_files` and the tree diff consistent\n * by construction (docs/architecture.md §7.2 convention 3).\n */\n\n/** Split file content into diffable lines (trailing newline does not create a phantom line). */\nfunction toLines(content) {\n  if (content === undefined || content === null || content === '') return [];\n  const raw = String(content).split('\\n');\n  if (raw.length > 1 && raw[raw.length - 1] === '') raw.pop();\n  return raw;\n}\n\n/**\n * LCS-based line diff. Returns the lines of `b` annotated with their origin:\n * `{type:'added'|'deleted'|'context', oldNumber, newNumber, text}`.\n * Numbers are 1-based; `null` marks the side a line does not belong to.\n */\nexport function diffLines(oldContent, newContent) {\n  const a = toLines(oldContent);\n  const b = toLines(newContent);\n  // Trim common prefix/suffix first: keeps the LCS table small for typical edits.\n  let start = 0;\n  while (start < a.length && start < b.length && a[start] === b[start]) start += 1;\n  let endA = a.length;\n  let endB = b.length;\n  while (endA > start && endB > start && a[endA - 1] === b[endB - 1]) {\n    endA -= 1;\n    endB -= 1;\n  }\n  const middleA = a.slice(start, endA);\n  const middleB = b.slice(start, endB);\n\n  // LCS table over the (usually small) middle section.\n  const rows = middleA.length;\n  const cols = middleB.length;\n  const lcs = Array.from({ length: rows + 1 }, () => new Array(cols + 1).fill(0));\n  for (let i = rows - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {\n    for (let j = cols - 1; j >= 0; j -= 1) {\n      lcs[i][j] = middleA[i] === middleB[j] ? lcs[i + 1][j + 1] + 1 : Math.max(lcs[i + 1][j], lcs[i][j + 1]);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** @type {Array<{type:string, oldNumber:number|null, newNumber:number|null, text:string}>} */\n  const middle = [];\n  let i = 0;\n  let j = 0;\n  while (i < rows && j < cols) {\n    if (middleA[i] === middleB[j]) {\n      middle.push({ type: 'context', oldNumber: start + i + 1, newNumber: start + j + 1, text: middleA[i] });\n      i += 1;\n      j += 1;\n    } else if (lcs[i + 1][j] >= lcs[i][j + 1]) {\n      middle.push({ type: 'deleted', oldNumber: start + i + 1, newNumber: null, text: middleA[i] });\n      i += 1;\n    } else {\n      middle.push({ type: 'added', oldNumber: null, newNumber: start + j + 1, text: middleB[j] });\n      j += 1;\n    }\n  }\n  while (i < rows) {\n    middle.push({ type: 'deleted', oldNumber: start + i + 1, newNumber: null, text: middleA[i] });\n    i += 1;\n  }\n  while (j < cols) {\n    middle.push({ type: 'added', oldNumber: null, newNumber: start + j + 1, text: middleB[j] });\n    j += 1;\n  }\n\n  const head = a.slice(0, start).map((text, index) => ({\n    type: 'context',\n    oldNumber: index + 1,\n    newNumber: index + 1,\n    text,\n  }));\n  const tail = a.slice(endA).map((text, index) => ({\n    type: 'context',\n    oldNumber: endA + index + 1,\n    newNumber: endB + index + 1,\n    text,\n  }));\n  return [...head, ...middle, ...tail];\n}\n\nfunction summarise(lines) {\n  let additions = 0;\n  let deletions = 0;\n  for (const line of lines) {\n    if (line.type === 'added') additions += 1;\n    else if (line.type === 'deleted') deletions += 1;\n  }\n  return { additions, deletions };\n}\n\n/**\n * Changed files between two trees, sorted by path.\n * @returns {Array<{path:string, status:'added'|'deleted'|'modified', additions:number, deletions:number, lines:Array}>}\n */\nexport function diffTrees(oldTree, newTree) {\n  const paths = new Set([...Object.keys(oldTree ?? {}), ...Object.keys(newTree ?? {})]);\n  const files = [];\n  for (const path of [...paths].sort()) {\n    const before = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(oldTree ?? {}, path) ? oldTree[path] : undefined;\n    const after = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(newTree ?? {}, path) ? newTree[path] : undefined;\n    if (before === after) continue;\n    const status = before === undefined ? 'added' : after === undefined ? 'deleted' : 'modified';\n    const lines = diffLines(status === 'added' ? '' : before, status === 'deleted' ? '' : after);\n    files.push({ path, status, ...summarise(lines), lines });\n  }\n  return files;\n}\n\n/** Same as {@link diffTrees} without the (potentially large) line payload. */\nexport function treeChangeSet(oldTree, newTree) {\n  return diffTrees(oldTree, newTree).map(({ path, status, additions, deletions }) => ({\n    path,\n    status,\n    additions,\n    deletions,\n  }));\n}\n\n/** Does a change set touch `path` itself or anything below it (directory scope)? */\nexport function changeSetTouchesPath(changeSet, path) {\n  if (!path) return changeSet.length > 0;\n  return changeSet.some((file) => file.path === path || file.path.startsWith(`${path}/`));\n}"
